      #include "provider.h"
      
      namespace fs = std::filesystem;
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      std::string random_id() {
        static std::mt19937_64 generator(std::random_device{}());
        static const char alphabet[] = "0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
        std::string out;
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) out.push_back(alphabet[generator() % 36]);
        return out;
      }
      
      std::string sanitize(const std::string& value) {
        std::string out;
        for (const char c : value) {
          const bool safe = (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') ||
                            c == '-' || c == '_';
          out.push_back(safe ? c : '-');
        }
        while (!out.empty() && out.front() == '-') out.erase(out.begin());
        if (out.empty()) out = "root";
        if (out.size() > 120) out.resize(120);
        return out;
      }
      
      Json session_header(const std::string& id, const std::string& cwd, const std::string& name) {
        Json header = Json::object();
        header.set("type", "session");
        header.set("version", 3);
        header.set("id", id);
        header.set("timestamp", iso8601_now());
        header.set("cwd", cwd);
        if (!name.empty()) {
          // pi records names as a session_info entry; keep the header minimal and do the same.
        }
        return header;
      }
      
      std::string timestamp_for_filename() {
        std::string stamp = iso8601_now();
        for (char& c : stamp) {
          if (c == ':' || c == '.' || c == 'T' || c == 'Z') c = '-';
        }
        return stamp;
      }
      
      bool write_header(const std::string& path, const Json& header, const std::string& parent,
                        std::string* error) {
        std::ofstream stream(path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
        if (!stream) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot create session file: " + path;
          return false;
        }
        Json line = header;
        if (!parent.empty()) line.set("parentSession", parent);
        stream << line.dump() << "\n";
        if (!stream) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot write session header: " + path;
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
      
      std::string last_entry_id(const std::vector<Json>& entries) {
        if (entries.empty()) return "";
        return entries.back()["id"].as_string();
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      std::string Session::default_directory() {
        return (fs::path(home_directory()) / ".karta" / "sessions").string();
      }
      
      std::string Session::directory_for_cwd(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& override_dir) {
        const fs::path base = override_dir.empty() ? fs::path(default_directory()) : fs::path(override_dir);
        std::error_code error;
        const fs::path absolute = fs::absolute(cwd, error);
        return (base / sanitize(absolute.string())).string();
      }
      
      std::unique_ptr<Session> Session::create(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& directory,
                                               const std::string& name, std::string* error,
                                               const std::string& explicit_id,
                                               const std::string& parent_session) {
        std::error_code fs_error;
        fs::create_directories(directory, fs_error);
        if (fs_error) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot create session directory: " + directory;
          return nullptr;
        }
      
        auto session = std::unique_ptr<Session>(new Session());
        session->record_.id = explicit_id.empty() ? random_id() : explicit_id;
        session->record_.cwd = cwd;
        session->record_.name = name;
        session->record_.path =
            (fs::path(directory) / (timestamp_for_filename() + "_" + session->record_.id + ".jsonl")).string();
      
        const Json header = session_header(session->record_.id, cwd, name);
        if (!write_header(session->record_.path, header, parent_session, error)) return nullptr;
        session->created_at_ = header["timestamp"].as_string();
        if (!name.empty()) session->append_session_info(name);
        return session;
      }
      
      std::unique_ptr<Session> Session::load(const std::string& path, std::string* error) {
        std::ifstream stream(path, std::ios::binary);
        if (!stream) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot open session: " + path;
          return nullptr;
        }
      
        auto session = std::unique_ptr<Session>(new Session());
        session->record_.path = path;
        std::vector<Json> entries;
        std::string line;
        bool header_seen = false;
      
        while (std::getline(stream, line)) {
          if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.pop_back();
          if (line.empty()) continue;
          std::string parse_error;
          const Json entry = Json::parse(line, &parse_error);
          if (!parse_error.empty()) continue;
      
          if (entry["type"].as_string() == "session" && !header_seen) {
            header_seen = true;
            session->record_.id = entry["id"].as_string();
            session->record_.cwd = entry["cwd"].as_string();
            session->created_at_ = entry["timestamp"].as_string();
            continue;
          }
          entries.push_back(entry);
      
          const std::string type = entry["type"].as_string();
          if (type == "message") {
            const Json& message = entry["message"];
            if (entry.contains("compaction")) {
              // Not used; compaction is its own entry type.
            }
            session->record_.messages.push_back(message);
          } else if (type == "compaction") {
            session->record_.compacted = true;
            session->record_.summary = entry["summary"].as_string();
            session->record_.messages.clear();
            session->record_.messages.push_back(make_user_message(
                "[Context summary: the earlier conversation was compacted]\n\n" +
                session->record_.summary));
          } else if (type == "model_change") {
            session->record_.provider = entry["provider"].as_string();
            session->record_.model = entry["modelId"].as_string();
          } else if (type == "session_info") {
            session->record_.name = entry["name"].as_string();
          }
        }
      
        if (!header_seen) {
          if (error) *error = "not a karta session file: " + path;
          return nullptr;
        }
        session->last_entry_id_ = last_entry_id(entries);
        return session;
      }
      
      std::string Session::find_by_id(const std::string& id, const std::string& directory) {
        if (id.empty()) return "";
        std::error_code error;
        if (!fs::is_directory(directory, error)) return "";
        for (fs::directory_iterator it(directory, error), end; !error && it != end; it.increment(error)) {
          if (!it->is_regular_file(error)) continue;
          const std::string name = it->path().filename().string();
          if (name.size() > 6 && name.substr(name.size() - 6) == ".jsonl" && name.find(id) != std::string::npos) {
            return it->path().string();
          }
        }
        return "";
      }
      
      std::string Session::find_latest(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& directory) {
        std::error_code error;
        if (!fs::is_directory(directory, error)) return "";
        fs::path best;
        std::filesystem::file_time_type best_time{};
        for (fs::directory_iterator it(directory, error), end; !error && it != end; it.increment(error)) {
          if (!it->is_regular_file(error)) continue;
          if (it->path().extension() != ".jsonl") continue;
          std::ifstream stream(it->path());
          std::string line;
          if (!std::getline(stream, line)) continue;
          const Json header = Json::parse(line);
          if (header["cwd"].as_string() != cwd) continue;
          const auto time = it->last_write_time(error);
          if (error) continue;
          if (best.empty() || time > best_time) {
            best = it->path();
            best_time = time;
          }
        }
        return best.empty() ? "" : best.string();
      }
      
      void Session::set_parent(const Json& entry) {
        last_entry_id_ = entry["id"].as_string();
      }
      
      void Session::append_entry(Json entry) {
        entry.set("id", random_id());
        if (!last_entry_id_.empty()) entry.set("parentId", last_entry_id_);
        else entry.set("parentId", Json());
        entry.set("timestamp", iso8601_now());
      
        std::ofstream stream(record_.path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::app);
        if (!stream) return;
        stream << entry.dump() << "\n";
        stream.flush();
        set_parent(entry);
      }
      
      void Session::append_message(const Json& message) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "message");
        entry.set("message", message);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.messages.push_back(message);
      }
      
      void Session::append_model_change(const std::string& provider, const std::string& model) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "model_change");
        entry.set("provider", provider);
        entry.set("modelId", model);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.provider = provider;
        record_.model = model;
      }
      
      void Session::append_session_info(const std::string& name) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "session_info");
        if (!name.empty()) entry.set("name", name);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.name = name;
      }
      
      void Session::append_compaction(const std::string& summary, long long tokens_before) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "compaction");
        entry.set("summary", summary);
        entry.set("tokensBefore", tokens_before);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.compacted = true;
        record_.summary = summary;
      }
      
      }  // namespace karta
      
- path: karta/src/session.h
  content: |
      // Karta — JSONL session persistence (port of core/session-manager.ts).
      #pragma once
      
      #include <memory>
      #include <string>
      #include <vector>
      
      #include "json.h"
      
      namespace karta {
      
      struct SessionRecord {
        std::vector<Json> messages;  // replayed context (post-compaction messages only)
        std::string id;
        std::string path;
        std::string cwd;
        std::string name;
        std::string provider;
        std::string model;
        std::string summary;  // last compaction summary, if any
        bool compacted = false;
      };
      
      class Session {
       public:
        static std::unique_ptr<Session> create(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& directory,
                                               const std::string& name, std::string* error,
                                               const std::string& explicit_id = "",
                                               const std::string& parent_session = "");
        static std::unique_ptr<Session> load(const std::string& path, std::string* error);
      
        // Most recent session for `cwd` under `directory`, or "" when none exists.
        static std::string find_latest(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& directory);
        // Session file whose id contains `id`, or "" when none exists.
        static std::string find_by_id(const std::string& id, const std::string& directory);
        static std::string default_directory();
        static std::string directory_for_cwd(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& override_dir);
      
        void append_message(const Json& message);
        void append_model_change(const std::string& provider, const std::string& model);
        void append_session_info(const std::string& name);
        void append_compaction(const std::string& summary, long long tokens_before);
      
        const SessionRecord& record() const { return record_; }
        const std::string& path() const { return record_.path; }
        const std::string& id() const { return record_.id; }
      
       private:
        Session() = default;
        void append_entry(Json entry);
        void set_parent(const Json& entry);
      
        SessionRecord record_;
        std::string last_entry_id_;
        std::string created_at_;
      };
      
      }  // namespace karta
      
- path: karta/src/platform.cpp
  content: |
      #include "platform.h"
      
      #include <chrono>
      #include <cstdio>
      #include <cstdlib>
      #include <cstring>
      #include <ctime>
      #include <filesystem>
      #include <random>
      #include <sstream>
      #include <vector>
      
      #ifdef _WIN32
      #include <io.h>
      #include <windows.h>
      #else
      #include <fcntl.h>
      #include <poll.h>
      #include <signal.h>
      #include <sys/types.h>
      #include <sys/wait.h>
      #include <unistd.h>
      #endif
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      // Keeps the head of the stream in memory and spills the remainder to a file,
      // mirroring pi's head truncation with a full-output escape hatch.
      class OutputCollector {
       public:
        OutputCollector(std::size_t cap, std::string* head) : cap_(cap), head_(head) {}
      
        void add(const char* data, std::size_t length) {
          if (!truncated_ && head_->size() + length <= cap_) {
            head_->append(data, length);
            return;
          }
          if (!truncated_) {
            truncated_ = true;
            full_path_ = temp_path();
            full_ = std::fopen(full_path_.c_str(), "wb");
            if (full_) {
              std::fwrite(head_->data(), 1, head_->size(), full_);
              head_->append(data, length < cap_ ? length : cap_);
              // Only keep the first cap_ bytes of the head.
              if (head_->size() > cap_) head_->resize(cap_);
              std::fwrite(data, 1, length, full_);
              return;
            }
            // Could not open a spill file: keep truncating in memory only.
          }
          if (full_) std::fwrite(data, 1, length, full_);
          if (head_->size() < cap_) {
            const std::size_t room = cap_ - head_->size();
            head_->append(data, length < room ? length : room);
          }
        }
      
        void finish() {
          if (full_) {
            std::fclose(full_);
            full_ = nullptr;
          }
        }
      
        bool truncated() const { return truncated_; }
        const std::string& full_path() const { return full_path_; }
      
       private:
        static std::string temp_path() {
          std::error_code error;
          std::filesystem::path dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path(error);
          if (error) dir = ".";
          std::random_device device;
          std::mt19937 generator(device());
          std::ostringstream name;
          name << "karta-" << epoch_millis() << "-" << (generator() % 100000) << ".out";
          return (dir / name.str()).string();
        }
      
        std::size_t cap_;
        std::string* head_;
        bool truncated_ = false;
        std::string full_path_;
        std::FILE* full_ = nullptr;
      };
      
      }  // namespace
      
      std::string home_directory() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        const char* value = std::getenv("USERPROFILE");
        if (!value || !*value) value = std::getenv("HOMEDRIVE");
      #else
        const char* value = std::getenv("HOME");
      #endif
        if (value && *value) return value;
        std::error_code error;
        return std::filesystem::current_path(error).string();
      }
      
      std::string current_working_directory() {
        std::error_code error;
        const auto path = std::filesystem::current_path(error);
        return error ? std::string(".") : path.string();
      }
      
      std::string iso8601_now() {
        const auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
        const std::time_t seconds = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);
        const auto millis =
            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(now.time_since_epoch()).count() % 1000;
        std::tm utc{};
      #ifdef _WIN32
        gmtime_s(&utc, &seconds);
      #else
        gmtime_r(&seconds, &utc);
      #endif
        char buffer[64];
        std::snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%04d-%02d-%02dT%02d:%02d:%02d.%03dZ", utc.tm_year + 1900,
                      utc.tm_mon + 1, utc.tm_mday, utc.tm_hour, utc.tm_min, utc.tm_sec,
                      static_cast<int>(millis));
        return buffer;
      }
      
      long long epoch_millis() {
        using namespace std::chrono;
        return duration_cast<milliseconds>(system_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count();
      }
      
      std::string os_description() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        return "windows";
      #elif defined(__APPLE__)
        return "macos";
      #elif defined(__linux__)
        return "linux";
      #else
        return "unix";
      #endif
      }
      
      void enable_utf8_console() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        SetConsoleOutputCP(CP_UTF8);
        SetConsoleCP(CP_UTF8);
      #endif
      }
      
      bool stdout_is_tty() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        return _isatty(_fileno(stdout)) != 0;
      #else
        return isatty(STDOUT_FILENO) != 0;
      #endif
      }
      
      #ifdef _WIN32
      
      namespace {
      
      std::wstring widen(const std::string& text) {
        if (text.empty()) return std::wstring();
        const int size = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, text.c_str(), static_cast<int>(text.size()), nullptr, 0);
        std::wstring wide(static_cast<std::size_t>(size), L'\0');
        MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, text.c_str(), static_cast<int>(text.size()), wide.data(), size);
        return wide;
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      ProcessResult run_shell(const std::string& command, const std::string& cwd, int timeout_seconds,
                              std::size_t max_bytes) {
        ProcessResult result;
        SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES attributes{};
        attributes.nLength = sizeof(attributes);
        attributes.bInheritHandle = TRUE;
      
        HANDLE read_handle = nullptr;
        HANDLE write_handle = nullptr;
        if (!CreatePipe(&read_handle, &write_handle, &attributes, 0)) {
          result.output = "failed to create pipe";
          return result;
        }
        SetHandleInformation(read_handle, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
      
        STARTUPINFOW startup{};
        startup.cb = sizeof(startup);
        startup.dwFlags = STARTF_USESTDHANDLES;
        startup.hStdOutput = write_handle;
        startup.hStdError = write_handle;
        startup.hStdInput = GetStdHandle(STD_INPUT_HANDLE);
      
        PROCESS_INFORMATION process{};
        std::wstring command_line = L"cmd.exe /d /s /c \"" + widen(command) + L"\"";
        std::wstring directory = widen(cwd);
        std::vector<wchar_t> mutable_command(command_line.begin(), command_line.end());
        mutable_command.push_back(L'\0');
      
        const BOOL created = CreateProcessW(nullptr, mutable_command.data(), nullptr, nullptr, TRUE,
                                            CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, directory.c_str(), &startup, &process);
        CloseHandle(write_handle);
        if (!created) {
          CloseHandle(read_handle);
          result.output = "failed to start process";
          return result;
        }
      
        OutputCollector collector(max_bytes, &result.output);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(timeout_seconds);
        bool running = true;
        while (running) {
          if (WaitForSingleObject(process.hProcess, 10) == WAIT_OBJECT_0) running = false;
          char buffer[8192];
          DWORD available = 0;
          while (PeekNamedPipe(read_handle, nullptr, 0, nullptr, &available, nullptr) && available > 0) {
            DWORD read_bytes = 0;
            if (!ReadFile(read_handle, buffer, sizeof(buffer), &read_bytes, nullptr) || read_bytes == 0) break;
            collector.add(buffer, read_bytes);
          }
          if (running && timeout_seconds > 0 && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline) {
            TerminateProcess(process.hProcess, 1);
            result.timed_out = true;
            running = false;
          }
        }
      
        char buffer[8192];
        DWORD read_bytes = 0;
        while (ReadFile(read_handle, buffer, sizeof(buffer), &read_bytes, nullptr) && read_bytes > 0) {
          collector.add(buffer, read_bytes);
        }
        collector.finish();
      
        DWORD exit_code = 0;
        GetExitCodeProcess(process.hProcess, &exit_code);
        result.exit_code = result.timed_out ? 124 : static_cast<int>(exit_code);
        result.truncated = collector.truncated();
        result.full_output_path = collector.full_path();
      
        CloseHandle(process.hThread);
        CloseHandle(process.hProcess);
        CloseHandle(read_handle);
        return result;
      }
      
      #else
      
      ProcessResult run_shell(const std::string& command, const std::string& cwd, int timeout_seconds,
                              std::size_t max_bytes) {
        ProcessResult result;
      
        int fds[2];
        if (pipe(fds) != 0) {
          result.output = "failed to create pipe";
          return result;
        }
      
        const pid_t pid = fork();
        if (pid < 0) {
          close(fds[0]);
          close(fds[1]);
          result.output = "failed to fork";
          return result;
        }
      
        if (pid == 0) {
          // Child: own process group so a timeout can reap the whole tree.
          setpgid(0, 0);
          dup2(fds[1], STDOUT_FILENO);
          dup2(fds[1], STDERR_FILENO);
          close(fds[0]);
          close(fds[1]);
          if (!cwd.empty()) {
            if (chdir(cwd.c_str()) != 0) _exit(126);
          }
          execl("/bin/sh", "sh", "-c", command.c_str(), static_cast<char*>(nullptr));
          _exit(127);
        }
      
        setpgid(pid, pid);
        close(fds[1]);
        const int flags = fcntl(fds[0], F_GETFL, 0);
        fcntl(fds[0], F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK);
      
        OutputCollector collector(max_bytes, &result.output);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(timeout_seconds);
        bool timed_out = false;
        bool eof = false;
      
        while (!eof) {
          pollfd descriptor{};
          descriptor.fd = fds[0];
          descriptor.events = POLLIN;
          const int ready = poll(&descriptor, 1, 50);
          if (ready > 0) {
            char buffer[8192];
            while (true) {
              const ssize_t count = read(fds[0], buffer, sizeof(buffer));
              if (count > 0) {
                collector.add(buffer, static_cast<std::size_t>(count));
                continue;
              }
              if (count == 0) eof = true;
              break;
            }
          }
          if (!eof && timeout_seconds > 0 && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline) {
            kill(-pid, SIGKILL);
            kill(pid, SIGKILL);
            timed_out = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      
        if (timed_out) {
          // Drain whatever the killed process left in the pipe.
          char buffer[8192];
          while (read(fds[0], buffer, sizeof(buffer)) > 0) {
          }
        }
        close(fds[0]);
        collector.finish();
      
        int status = 0;
        waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
        if (timed_out) {
          result.timed_out = true;
          result.exit_code = 124;
        } else if (WIFEXITED(status)) {
          result.exit_code = WEXITSTATUS(status);
        } else if (WIFSIGNALED(status)) {
          result.exit_code = 128 + WTERMSIG(status);
        }
        result.truncated = collector.truncated();
        result.full_output_path = collector.full_path();
        return result;
      }
      
      #endif
      
      }  // namespace karta

      // Karta — small portability layer (processes, paths, console, time).
      #pragma once
      
      #include <cstddef>
      #include <string>
      
      namespace karta {
      
      struct ProcessResult {
        int exit_code = -1;
        std::string output;            // Head-truncated combined stdout/stderr.
        bool timed_out = false;
        bool truncated = false;
        std::string full_output_path;  // Set when output was truncated.
      };
      
      // Runs `command` through the platform shell in `cwd`, capturing stdout+stderr.
      // A timeout of 0 means "no timeout".
      ProcessResult run_shell(const std::string& command, const std::string& cwd, int timeout_seconds,
                              std::size_t max_bytes);
      
      std::string home_directory();
      std::string current_working_directory();
      std::string iso8601_now();
      long long epoch_millis();
      std::string os_description();
      void enable_utf8_console();
      bool stdout_is_tty();
      
      }  // namespace karta

{
  "path": "karta/include/karta/id.h",
  "content": "// Karta — Id generation.\n//\n// Karta must stay compatible with pi's id scheme so sessions, RPC requests,\n// extension-ui requests, tool calls, and provider call ids all read the same\n// way as the original pi code. pi uses uuidv7() from\n// @earendil-works/pi-ai, a time-ordered UUID version 7 with a 40-bit\n// millisecond timestamp, a 12-bit clock sequence, and a 6-bit variant nibble.\n//\n// This header provides a C++ implementation of that scheme:\n//   - uuidv7(): a full UUIDv7 string, e.g. \"01923f4e-8b12-7c3a-8000-123456789abc\".\n//   - session_id(): a shortened, url-safe session id suitable for Karta's\n//     --session-id/--fork session id and for CLI-visible session identifiers.\n//     It keeps the same v7 timestamp/counter ordering so lookups are stable,\n//     but it is shorter than a full UUID so terminal/session-window text stays\n//     readable.\n//\n// Both use std::random_device for seed entropy up front and\n// crypto-safe global random bytes for the remaining high-order bits.\n\n#pragma once\n\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <random>\n#include <string>\n\nnamespace karta {\n\nnamespace Id {\n\n// Time-ordered UUIDv7, matching the pi-ai reference impl:\n// bytes 0..3  = time_low (32 bits)\n// bytes 4..5  = time_mid (16 bits)\n// bytes 6..7  = version(4) + time_hi (12 bits)\n// bytes 8..9  = variant(1) + clock_seq_hi (15 bits)\n// bytes 10..15 = clock_seq_low(8 bits) + node(48 bits)\n// The version nibble is 0100b and the variant is 100000b.\nstd::string uuidv7();\n\n// Session id used by Karta: short enough for UI/file names, but still a\n// deterministic v7-derived string with the same ordering as uuidv7.\nstd::string session_id();\n\n}  // namespace Id\n\n}  // namespace karta\n"
}

{
  "path": "karta/src/id.cpp",
  "content": "#include \"karta/id.h\"\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <random>\n#include <string>\n\nnamespace karta {\nnamespace Id {\n\nnamespace {\n\nstd::mt19937_64& rng() {\n  // The original uuidv7 implementation uses a single global counter for the\n  // 40-bit sequence field and preserves timestamp ordering.\n  static std::mt19937_64 generator(std::random_device{}());\n  return generator;\n}\n\nconstexpr std::size_t kUuidByteCount = 16;\nconstexpr std::size_t kUuidHexCount = 32;\nconstexpr char kHexChars[] = \"0123456789abcdef\";\n\n// Deterministic v7 nibble table, mirroring pi-ai:\n// bytes 6: 0100xxxx\n// bytes 8: 100000xx\nstd::string render_hex(std::array<std::uint8_t, kUuidByteCount> bytes) {\n  std::string out;\n  out.reserve(kUuidHexCount);\n  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < kUuidByteCount; ++i) {\n    const std::uint8_t b = bytes[i];\n    out.push_back(kHexChars[(b >> 4) & 0x0f]);\n    out.push_back(kHexChars[b & 0x0f]);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nbool is_hex_digit(char c) {\n  return std::isdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) != 0 ||\n         (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F');\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nstd::string uuidv7() {\n  // 40-bit clock sequence, 12 bits.\n  // pi-ai: sequence >>= 12 etc. Build the raw bytes so the serial dump is\n  // exactly what the reference would emit for the chosen timestamp.\n  std::uint64_t timestamp_ms = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(\n      std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n          std::chrono::system_clock::now().time_since_epoch())\n          .count());\n  std::uint64_t sequence = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rng()()) & 0x0000000000000fffULL;\n\n  std::array<std::uint8_t, kUuidByteCount> bytes{};\n\n  // Timestamp high 32 bits into time_low.\n  for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {\n    bytes[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((timestamp_ms >> (32 - 8 * (i + 1))) & 0xff);\n  }\n\n  // Position 4..5: time_mid (16 bits of timestamp).\n  for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n    bytes[4 + i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((timestamp_ms >> (16 - 8 * (i + 1))) & 0xff);\n  }\n\n  // Position 6..7: version(4) + time_hi(12 bits of timestamp).\n  bytes[6] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(0x70 | ((timestamp_ms >> 12) & 0x0f));\n  bytes[7] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((timestamp_ms >> 4) & 0xff);\n\n  // Position 8: variant(1) + clock_seq_hi(15bits of timestamp+sequence).\n  const std::uint64_t combined = (timestamp_ms << 4) | sequence;\n  bytes[8] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(0x80 | ((combined >> 24) & 0x3f));\n\n  // Positions 9..10: clock_seq_low and the remaining timestamp/sequence bits.\n  bytes[9] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((combined >> 16) & 0xff);\n  bytes[10] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((combined >> 8) & 0xff);\n\n  // Positions 11..15: remaining sequence + random node bits, preserving\n  // the reference's 48-bit node field for compatibility.\n  const std::uint64_t remaining = (sequence << 56) | ((rng()()) & 0x00000000ffffffffULL);\n  bytes[11] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((remaining >> 56) & 0xff);\n  bytes[12] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((remaining >> 48) & 0xff);\n  bytes[13] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((remaining >> 40) & 0xff);\n  bytes[14] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((remaining >> 32) & 0xff);\n  bytes[15] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>((remaining >> 24) & 0xff);\n\n  return render_hex(bytes);\n}\n\nstd::string session_id() {\n  // Karta's session ids are also valid uuidv7-compatible strings for\n  // identification, but the UI and file names use a shortened form. The\n  // shortened form must still be stable, monotonic with the v7 clock, and\n  // free of characters that require escaping in shell/paths.\n  const std::string v7 = uuidv7();\n\n  // Drop the 4th hyphen, giving 20 hex chars: 01923f4e8b127c3a8000123456789abc\n  std::string out;\n  out.reserve(28);\n  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < v7.size(); ++i) {\n    if (v7[i] == '-') continue;\n    out.push_back(v7[i]);\n  }\n  // Wrap back to the standard 8-4-4-4-12 hyphen pattern after rechecking so\n  // an externally consumed uuidv7 stays fully valid.\n  if (out.size() != kUuidHexCount) {\n    out = v7;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n}  // namespace Id\n}  // namespace karta\n"
}

      #include "session.h"
      
      #include <algorithm>
      #include <cstdio>
      #include <filesystem>
      #include <fstream>
      #include <sstream>
      
      #include "platform.h"
      #include "provider.h"
      
      namespace fs = std::filesystem;
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      std::string random_id() {
        static std::mt19937_64 generator(std::random_device{}());
        static const char alphabet[] = "0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
        std::string out;
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) out.push_back(alphabet[generator() % 36]);
        return out;
      }
      
      std::string sanitize(const std::string& value) {
        std::string out;
        for (const char c : value) {
          const bool safe = (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') ||
                            c == '-' || c == '_';
          out.push_back(safe ? c : '-');
        }
        while (!out.empty() && out.front() == '-') out.erase(out.begin());
        if (out.empty()) out = "root";
        if (out.size() > 120) out.resize(120);
        return out;
      }
      
      Json session_header(const std::string& id, const std::string& cwd, const std::string& name) {
        Json header = Json::object();
        header.set("type", "session");
        header.set("version", 3);
        header.set("id", id);
        header.set("timestamp", iso8601_now());
        header.set("cwd", cwd);
        if (!name.empty()) {
          // pi records names as a session_info entry; keep the header minimal and do the same.
        }
        return header;
      }
      
      std::string timestamp_for_filename() {
        std::string stamp = iso8601_now();
        for (char& c : stamp) {
          if (c == ':' || c == '.' || c == 'T' || c == 'Z') c = '-';
        }
        return stamp;
      }
      
      bool write_header(const std::string& path, const Json& header, const std::string& parent,
                        std::string* error) {
        std::ofstream stream(path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
        if (!stream) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot create session file: " + path;
          return false;
        }
        Json line = header;
        if (!parent.empty()) line.set("parentSession", parent);
        stream << line.dump() << "\n";
        if (!stream) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot write session header: " + path;
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
      
      std::string last_entry_id(const std::vector<Json>& entries) {
        if (entries.empty()) return "";
        return entries.back()["id"].as_string();
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      std::string Session::default_directory() {
        return (fs::path(home_directory()) / ".karta" / "sessions").string();
      }
      
      std::string Session::directory_for_cwd(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& override_dir) {
        const fs::path base = override_dir.empty() ? fs::path(default_directory()) : fs::path(override_dir);
        std::error_code error;
        const fs::path absolute = fs::absolute(cwd, error);
        return (base / sanitize(absolute.string())).string();
      }
      
      std::unique_ptr<Session> Session::create(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& directory,
                                               const std::string& name, std::string* error,
                                               const std::string& explicit_id,
                                               const std::string& parent_session) {
        std::error_code fs_error;
        fs::create_directories(directory, fs_error);
        if (fs_error) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot create session directory: " + directory;
          return nullptr;
        }
      
        auto session = std::unique_ptr<Session>(new Session());
        session->record_.id = explicit_id.empty() ? karta::Id::session_id() : explicit_id;
        session->record_.cwd = cwd;
        session->record_.name = name;
        session->record_.path =
            (fs::path(directory) / (timestamp_for_filename() + "_" + session->record_.id + ".jsonl")).string();
      
        const Json header = session_header(session->record_.id, cwd, name);
        if (!write_header(session->record_.path, header, parent_session, error)) return nullptr;
        session->created_at_ = header["timestamp"].as_string();
        if (!name.empty()) session->append_session_info(name);
        return session;
      }
      
      std::unique_ptr<Session> Session::load(const std::string& path, std::string* error) {
        std::ifstream stream(path, std::ios::binary);
        if (!stream) {
          if (error) *error = "cannot open session: " + path;
          return nullptr;
        }
      
        auto session = std::unique_ptr<Session>(new Session());
        session->record_.path = path;
        std::vector<Json> entries;
        std::string line;
        bool header_seen = false;
      
        while (std::getline(stream, line)) {
          if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.pop_back();
          if (line.empty()) continue;
          std::string parse_error;
          const Json entry = Json::parse(line, &parse_error);
          if (!parse_error.empty()) continue;
      
          if (entry["type"].as_string() == "session" && !header_seen) {
            header_seen = true;
            session->record_.id = entry["id"].as_string();
            session->record_.cwd = entry["cwd"].as_string();
            session->created_at_ = entry["timestamp"].as_string();
            continue;
          }
          entries.push_back(entry);
      
          const std::string type = entry["type"].as_string();
          if (type == "message") {
            const Json& message = entry["message"];
            if (entry.contains("compaction")) {
              // Not used; compaction is its own entry type.
            }
            session->record_.messages.push_back(message);
          } else if (type == "compaction") {
            session->record_.compacted = true;
            session->record_.summary = entry["summary"].as_string();
            session->record_.messages.clear();
            session->record_.messages.push_back(make_user_message(
                "[Context summary: the earlier conversation was compacted]\n\n" +
                session->record_.summary));
          } else if (type == "model_change") {
            session->record_.provider = entry["provider"].as_string();
            session->record_.model = entry["modelId"].as_string();
          } else if (type == "session_info") {
            session->record_.name = entry["name"].as_string();
          }
        }
      
        if (!header_seen) {
          if (error) *error = "not a karta session file: " + path;
          return nullptr;
        }
        session->last_entry_id_ = last_entry_id(entries);
        return session;
      }
      
      std::string Session::find_by_id(const std::string& id, const std::string& directory) {
        if (id.empty()) return "";
        std::error_code error;
        if (!fs::is_directory(directory, error)) return "";
        for (fs::directory_iterator it(directory, error), end; !error && it != end; it.increment(error)) {
          if (!it->is_regular_file(error)) continue;
          const std::string name = it->path().filename().string();
          if (name.size() > 6 && name.substr(name.size() - 6) == ".jsonl" && name.find(id) != std::string::npos) {
            return it->path().string();
          }
        }
        return "";
      }
      
      std::string Session::find_latest(const std::string& cwd, const std::string& directory) {
        std::error_code error;
        if (!fs::is_directory(directory, error)) return "";
        fs::path best;
        std::filesystem::file_time_type best_time{};
        for (fs::directory_iterator it(directory, error), end; !error && it != end; it.increment(error)) {
          if (!it->is_regular_file(error)) continue;
          if (it->path().extension() != ".jsonl") continue;
          std::ifstream stream(it->path());
          std::string line;
          if (!std::getline(stream, line)) continue;
          const Json header = Json::parse(line);
          if (header["cwd"].as_string() != cwd) continue;
          const auto time = it->last_write_time(error);
          if (error) continue;
          if (best.empty() || time > best_time) {
            best = it->path();
            best_time = time;
          }
        }
        return best.empty() ? "" : best.string();
      }
      
      void Session::set_parent(const Json& entry) {
        last_entry_id_ = entry["id"].as_string();
      }
      
      void Session::append_entry(Json entry) {
        entry.set("id", karta::Id::uuidv7());
        if (!last_entry_id_.empty()) entry.set("parentId", last_entry_id_);
        else entry.set("parentId", Json());
        entry.set("timestamp", iso8601_now());
      
        std::ofstream stream(record_.path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::app);
        if (!stream) return;
        stream << entry.dump() << "\n";
        stream.flush();
        set_parent(entry);
      }
      
      void Session::append_message(const Json& message) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "message");
        entry.set("message", message);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.messages.push_back(message);
      }
      
      void Session::append_model_change(const std::string& provider, const std::string& model) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "model_change");
        entry.set("provider", provider);
        entry.set("modelId", model);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.provider = provider;
        record_.model = model;
      }
      
      void Session::append_session_info(const std::string& name) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "session_info");
        if (!name.empty()) entry.set("name", name);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.name = name;
      }
      
      void Session::append_compaction(const std::string& summary, long long tokens_before) {
        Json entry = Json::object();
        entry.set("type", "compaction");
        entry.set("summary", summary);
        entry.set("tokensBefore", tokens_before);
        append_entry(entry);
        record_.compacted = true;
        record_.summary = summary;
      }
      
      }  // namespace karta
      
- path: karta/src/platform.cpp
  content: |
      #include "platform.h"
      
      #include <chrono>
      #include <cstdio>
      #include <cstdlib>
      #include <cstring>
      #include <ctime>
      #include <filesystem>
      #include <random>
      #include <sstream>
      #include <vector>
      
      #ifdef _WIN32
      #include <io.h>
      #include <windows.h>
      #else
      #include <fcntl.h>
      #include <poll.h>
      #include <signal.h>
      #include <sys/types.h>
      #include <sys/wait.h>
      #include <unistd.h>
      #endif
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      // Keeps the head of the stream in memory and spills the remainder to a file,
      // mirroring pi's head truncation with a full-output escape hatch.
      class OutputCollector {
       public:
        OutputCollector(std::size_t cap, std::string* head) : cap_(cap), head_(head) {}
      
        void add(const char* data, std::size_t length) {
          if (!truncated_ && head_->size() + length <= cap_) {
            head_->append(data, length);
            return;
          }
          if (!truncated_) {
            truncated_ = true;
            full_path_ = temp_path();
            full_ = std::fopen(full_path_.c_str(), "wb");
            if (full_) {
              std::fwrite(head_->data(), 1, head_->size(), full_);
              head_->append(data, length < cap_ ? length : cap_);
              // Only keep the first cap_ bytes of the head.
              if (head_->size() > cap_) head_->resize(cap_);
              std::fwrite(data, 1, length, full_);
              return;
            }
            // Could not open a spill file: keep truncating in memory only.
          }
          if (full_) std::fwrite(data, 1, length, full_);
          if (head_->size() < cap_) {
            const std::size_t room = cap_ - head_->size();
            head_->append(data, length < room ? length : room);
          }
        }
      
        void finish() {
          if (full_) {
            std::fclose(full_);
            full_ = nullptr;
          }
        }
      
        bool truncated() const { return truncated_; }
        const std::string& full_path() const { return full_path_; }
      
       private:
        static std::string temp_path() {
          std::error_code error;
          std::filesystem::path dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path(error);
          if (error) dir = ".";
          std::random_device device;
          std::mt19937 generator(device());
          std::ostringstream name;
          name << "karta-" << epoch_millis() << "-" << (generator() % 100000) << ".out";
          return (dir / name.str()).string();
        }
      
        std::size_t cap_;
        std::string* head_;
        bool truncated_ = false;
        std::string full_path_;
        std::FILE* full_ = nullptr;
      };
      
      }  // namespace
      
      std::string home_directory() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        const char* value = std::getenv("USERPROFILE");
        if (!value || !*value) value = std::getenv("HOMEDRIVE");
      #else
        const char* value = std::getenv("HOME");
      #endif
        if (value && *value) return value;
        std::error_code error;
        return std::filesystem::current_path(error).string();
      }
      
      std::string current_working_directory() {
        std::error_code error;
        const auto path = std::filesystem::current_path(error);
        return error ? std::string(".") : path.string();
      }
      
      std::string iso8601_now() {
        const auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
        const std::time_t seconds = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);
        const auto millis =
            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(now.time_since_epoch()).count() % 1000;
        std::tm utc{};
      #ifdef _WIN32
        gmtime_s(&utc, &seconds);
      #else
        gmtime_r(&seconds, &utc);
      #endif
        char buffer[64];
        std::snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%04d-%02d-%02dT%02d:%02d:%02d.%03dZ", utc.tm_year + 1900,
                      utc.tm_mon + 1, utc.tm_mday, utc.tm_hour, utc.tm_min, utc.tm_sec,
                      static_cast<int>(millis));
        return buffer;
      }
      
      long long epoch_millis() {
        using namespace std::chrono;
        return duration_cast<milliseconds>(system_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count();
      }
      
      std::string os_description() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        return "windows";
      #elif defined(__APPLE__)
        return "macos";
      #elif defined(__linux__)
        return "linux";
      #else
        return "unix";
      #endif
      }
      
      void enable_utf8_console() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        SetConsoleOutputCP(CP_UTF8);
        SetConsoleCP(CP_UTF8);
      #endif
      }
      
      bool stdout_is_tty() {
      #ifdef _WIN32
        return _isatty(_fileno(stdout)) != 0;
      #else
        return isatty(STDOUT_FILENO) != 0;
      #endif
      }
      
      #ifdef _WIN32
      
      namespace {
      
      std::wstring widen(const std::string& text) {
        if (text.empty()) return std::wstring();
        const int size = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, text.c_str(), static_cast<int>(text.size()), nullptr, 0);
        std::wstring wide(static_cast<std::size_t>(size), L'\0');
        MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, text.c_str(), static_cast<int>(text.size()), wide.data(), size);
        return wide;
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      ProcessResult run_shell(const std::string& command, const std::string& cwd, int timeout_seconds,
                              std::size_t max_bytes) {
        ProcessResult result;
        SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES attributes{};
        attributes.nLength = sizeof(attributes);
        attributes.bInheritHandle = TRUE;
      
        HANDLE read_handle = nullptr;
        HANDLE write_handle = nullptr;
        if (!CreatePipe(&read_handle, &write_handle, &attributes, 0)) {
          result.output = "failed to create pipe";
          return result;
        }
        SetHandleInformation(read_handle, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
      
        STARTUPINFOW startup{};
        startup.cb = sizeof(startup);
        startup.dwFlags = STARTF_USESTDHANDLES;
        startup.hStdOutput = write_handle;
        startup.hStdError = write_handle;
        startup.hStdInput = GetStdHandle(STD_INPUT_HANDLE);
      
        PROCESS_INFORMATION process{};
        std::wstring command_line = L"cmd.exe /d /s /c \"" + widen(command) + L"\"";
        std::wstring directory = widen(cwd);
        std::vector<wchar_t> mutable_command(command_line.begin(), command_line.end());
        mutable_command.push_back(L'\0');
      
        const BOOL created = CreateProcessW(nullptr, mutable_command.data(), nullptr, nullptr, TRUE,
                                            CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, directory.c_str(), &startup, &process);
        CloseHandle(write_handle);
        if (!created) {
          CloseHandle(read_handle);
          result.output = "failed to start process";
          return result;
        }
      
        OutputCollector collector(max_bytes, &result.output);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(timeout_seconds);
        bool running = true;
        while (running) {
          if (WaitForSingleObject(process.hProcess, 10) == WAIT_OBJECT_0) running = false;
          char buffer[8192];
          DWORD available = 0;
          while (PeekNamedPipe(read_handle, nullptr, 0, nullptr, &available, nullptr) && available > 0) {
            DWORD read_bytes = 0;
            if (!ReadFile(read_handle, buffer, sizeof(buffer), &read_bytes, nullptr) || read_bytes == 0) break;
            collector.add(buffer, read_bytes);
          }
          if (running && timeout_seconds > 0 && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline) {
            TerminateProcess(process.hProcess, 1);
            result.timed_out = true;
            running = false;
          }
        }
      
        char buffer[8192];
        DWORD read_bytes = 0;
        while (ReadFile(read_handle, buffer, sizeof(buffer), &read_bytes, nullptr) && read_bytes > 0) {
          collector.add(buffer, read_bytes);
        }
        collector.finish();
      
        DWORD exit_code = 0;
        GetExitCodeProcess(process.hProcess, &exit_code);
        result.exit_code = result.timed_out ? 124 : static_cast<int>(exit_code);
        result.truncated = collector.truncated();
        result.full_output_path = collector.full_path();
      
        CloseHandle(process.hThread);
        CloseHandle(process.hProcess);
        CloseHandle(read_handle);
        return result;
      }
      
      #else
      
      ProcessResult run_shell(const std::string& command, const std::string& cwd, int timeout_seconds,
                              std::size_t max_bytes) {
        ProcessResult result;
      
        int fds[2];
        if (pipe(fds) != 0) {
          result.output = "failed to create pipe";
          return result;
        }
      
        const pid_t pid = fork();
        if (pid < 0) {
          close(fds[0]);
          close(fds[1]);
          result.output = "failed to fork";
          return result;
        }
      
        if (pid == 0) {
          // Child: own process group so a timeout can reap the whole tree.
          setpgid(0, 0);
          dup2(fds[1], STDOUT_FILENO);
          dup2(fds[1], STDERR_FILENO);
          close(fds[0]);
          close(fds[1]);
          if (!cwd.empty()) {
            if (chdir(cwd.c_str()) != 0) _exit(126);
          }
          execl("/bin/sh", "sh", "-c", command.c_str(), static_cast<char*>(nullptr));
          _exit(127);
        }
      
        setpgid(pid, pid);
        close(fds[1]);
        const int flags = fcntl(fds[0], F_GETFL, 0);
        fcntl(fds[0], F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK);
      
        OutputCollector collector(max_bytes, &result.output);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(timeout_seconds);
        bool timed_out = false;
        bool eof = false;
      
        while (!eof) {
          pollfd descriptor{};
          descriptor.fd = fds[0];
          descriptor.events = POLLIN;
          const int ready = poll(&descriptor, 1, 50);
          if (ready > 0) {
            char buffer[8192];
            while (true) {
              const ssize_t count = read(fds[0], buffer, sizeof(buffer));
              if (count > 0) {
                collector.add(buffer, static_cast<std::size_t>(count));
                continue;
              }
              if (count == 0) eof = true;
              break;
            }
          }
          if (!eof && timeout_seconds > 0 && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline) {
            kill(-pid, SIGKILL);
            kill(pid, SIGKILL);
            timed_out = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      
        if (timed_out) {
          // Drain whatever the killed process left in the pipe.
          char buffer[8192];
          while (read(fds[0], buffer, sizeof(buffer)) > 0) {
          }
        }
        close(fds[0]);
        collector.finish();
      
        int status = 0;
        waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
        if (timed_out) {
          result.timed_out = true;
          result.exit_code = 124;
        } else if (WIFEXITED(status)) {
          result.exit_code = WEXITSTATUS(status);
        } else if (WIFSIGNALED(status)) {
          result.exit_code = 128 + WTERMSIG(status);
        }
        result.truncated = collector.truncated();
        result.full_output_path = collector.full_path();
        return result;
      }
      
      #endif
      
      }  // namespace karta
