```
  content: |
      #include "cli.h"
      
      #include <algorithm>
      #include <atomic>
      #include <condition_variable>
      #include <csignal>
      #include <cstdio>
      #include <cstdlib>
      #include <deque>
      #include <iostream>
      #include <memory>
      #include <mutex>
      #include <sstream>
      #include <thread>
      
      #include "agent.h"
      #include "auth.h"
      #include "json.h"
      #include "platform.h"
      #include "prompt.h"
      #include "provider.h"
      #include "session.h"
      #include "tools.h"
      
      #ifdef KARTA_TUI
      #include "tui/app.h"
      #endif
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      constexpr const char* kVersion = "0.1.0";
      
      std::atomic<bool>* g_interrupt_target = nullptr;
      
      void handle_sigint(int) {
        if (g_interrupt_target) g_interrupt_target->store(true);
      }
      
      bool matches_tool_pattern(const std::string& pattern, const std::string& name) {
        if (pattern == "*") return true;
        if (!pattern.empty() && pattern.back() == '*') {
          return name.rfind(pattern.substr(0, pattern.size() - 1), 0) == 0;
        }
        return pattern == name;
      }
      
      bool tool_allowed(const std::string& name, const CliOptions& options) {
        if (options.no_tools) return false;
        if (!options.tool_allow.empty()) {
          bool allowed = false;
          for (const std::string& pattern : options.tool_allow) {
            if (matches_tool_pattern(pattern, name)) allowed = true;
          }
          if (!allowed) return false;
        }
        for (const std::string& pattern : options.tool_exclude) {
          if (matches_tool_pattern(pattern, name)) return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
      
      std::string trim(const std::string& value) {
        const std::size_t begin = value.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
        if (begin == std::string::npos) return "";
        const std::size_t end = value.find_last_not_of(" \t\r\n");
        return value.substr(begin, end - begin + 1);
      }
      
      std::string first_line(const std::string& text, std::size_t limit) {
        const std::size_t newline = text.find('\n');
        std::string line = newline == std::string::npos ? text : text.substr(0, newline);
        if (line.size() > limit) line = line.substr(0, limit) + "…";
        return line;
      }
      
      class Printer {
       public:
        Printer(const CliOptions& options, bool interactive)
            : options_(options), interactive_(interactive) {}
      
        void event(const AgentEvent& event) {
          if (options_.mode == "text") {
            text_event(event);
          } else {
            json_event(event);
          }
        }
      
        void banner(const std::string& model, const std::string& cwd) {
          if (options_.mode != "text" || !interactive_) return;
          std::cout << "karta " << kVersion << " · " << model << " · " << cwd << "\n";
          std::cout << "Ctrl+C cancels a turn · /exit quits · /session shows the session file\n";
        }
      
       private:
        void text_event(const AgentEvent& event) {
          if (event.type == "message_update") {
            std::cout << event.payload["delta"].as_string() << std::flush;
          } else if (event.type == "tool_execution_start" && interactive_) {
            std::cout << "\n\n● " << event.payload["toolName"].as_string() << " "
                      << first_line(event.payload["arguments"].dump(), 120) << "\n";
            std::cout.flush();
          } else if (event.type == "tool_execution_end" && interactive_) {
            const std::string result = event.payload["result"].as_string();
            std::cout << "  " << (event.payload["isError"].as_bool(false) ? "✗ " : "✓ ")
                      << first_line(result, 160) << "\n";
            std::cout.flush();
          } else if (event.type == "error") {
            std::cerr << "\nerror: " << event.payload["message"].as_string() << "\n";
          } else if (event.type == "compaction") {
            if (interactive_) std::cerr << "\n[compacted conversation: " << event.payload["tokensBefore"].as_int(0)
                                        << " tokens]\n";
          } else if (event.type == "compaction_failed") {
            std::cerr << "\n[compaction failed: " << event.payload["message"].as_string() << "]\n";
          }
        }
      
        void json_event(const AgentEvent& event) {
          Json out = Json::object();
          out.set("type", event.type);
          if (event.type == "message_start" || event.type == "message_end") {
            out.set("message", event.payload);
          } else if (event.type == "message_update") {
            out.set("delta", event.payload["delta"]);
          } else if (event.type == "turn_end") {
            out.set("message", event.payload["message"]);
            out.set("toolResults", event.payload["toolResults"]);
          } else if (event.type == "error" || event.type == "compaction_failed") {
            out.set("message", event.payload["message"]);
          } else if (event.payload.is_object()) {
            for (const auto& field : event.payload.fields()) out.set(field.first, field.second);
          }
          std::cout << out.dump() << "\n";
          std::cout.flush();
        }
      
        const CliOptions& options_;
        bool interactive_;
      };
      
      std::string version_string() {
        std::ostringstream out;
        out << "karta " << kVersion;
        return out.str();
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      void print_help() {
        std::cout <<
            "karta " << kVersion << " — a coding agent (C++ port of pi)\n\n"
            "Usage: karta [options] [prompt]\n\n"
            "Running karta with no prompt opens the terminal UI. Use /login to connect a\n"
            "provider with an API key and /model to pick from that provider's models.\n\n"
            "Options:\n"
            "  --provider <name>              Provider: anthropic, openai, openrouter, groq,\n"
            "                                 deepseek, xai, mistral, nvidia, ollama, lmstudio\n"
            "  --model <pattern>              Model id, or \"provider/model\"\n"
            "  --api-key <key>                API key (defaults to the provider's env var)\n"
            "  --system-prompt <text>         Replace the default system prompt\n"
            "  --append-system-prompt <text>  Append to the system prompt (repeatable)\n"
            "  --mode <mode>                  Output: text (default), json, or rpc\n"
            "  -p, --print                    Non-interactive: run the prompt and exit\n"
            "  --tui                          Force the terminal UI (default on a TTY)\n"
            "  -c, --continue                 Continue the most recent session for this directory\n"
            "  --session <path>               Open a session by file path\n"
            "  --session-id <id>              Use or create a session with this id\n"
            "  --fork <path>                  Fork a session into this directory\n"
            "  --session-dir <dir>            Session storage directory\n"
            "  --no-session                   Do not persist the session\n"
            "  -n, --name <name>              Session display name\n"
            "  -t, --tools <list>             Comma-separated tool allowlist (supports *)\n"
            "  -xt, --exclude-tools <list>    Comma-separated tool denylist\n"
            "  -nt, --no-tools                Disable all tools\n"
            "  -nbt, --no-builtin-tools       Disable built-in tools\n"
            "  --thinking <level>             off, minimal, low, medium, high, xhigh, max\n"
            "  --max-turns <n>                Stop after n model turns (0 = unlimited)\n"
            "  --compact-threshold <tokens>   Auto-compact above this estimate (0 = off)\n"
            "  --list-models                  Show providers and default models\n"
            "  --verbose                      Print session and model details\n"
            "  -v, --version                  Print the version\n"
            "  -h, --help                     Print this help\n\n"
            "Environment:\n"
            "  ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY, OPENROUTER_API_KEY, GROQ_API_KEY,\n"
            "  DEEPSEEK_API_KEY, XAI_API_KEY, MISTRAL_API_KEY, NVIDIA_API_KEY, or KARTA_API_KEY\n"
            "  KARTA_BASE_URL, KARTA_MODEL, KARTA_MAX_TOKENS, KARTA_THINKING\n\n"
            "Credentials entered with /login are stored in " << auth_file_path() << " (0600).\n";
      }
      
      CliOptions parse_args(int argc, char** argv) {
        CliOptions options;
        auto need_value = [&](int& index, const std::string& flag) -> std::string {
          if (index + 1 >= argc) {
            std::cerr << "error: " << flag << " requires a value\n";
            std::exit(2);
          }
          return argv[++index];
        };
        auto split_list = [](const std::string& value) {
          std::vector<std::string> items;
          std::string current;
          std::istringstream stream(value);
          while (std::getline(stream, current, ',')) {
            const std::string item = trim(current);
            if (!item.empty()) items.push_back(item);
          }
          return items;
        };
      
        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
          std::string arg = argv[i];
          if (arg == "--help" || arg == "-h") {
            options.help = true;
          } else if (arg == "--version" || arg == "-v") {
            options.version = true;
          } else if (arg == "--list-models") {
            options.list_models = true;
          } else if (arg == "--mode") {
            options.mode = need_value(i, arg);
            if (options.mode != "text" && options.mode != "json" && options.mode != "rpc") {
              std::cerr << "error: --mode requires text, json, or rpc\n";
              std::exit(2);
            }
          } else if (arg == "--provider") {
            options.provider = need_value(i, arg);
            options.provider_set = true;
          } else if (arg == "--model") {
            options.model = need_value(i, arg);
            options.model_set = true;
          } else if (arg == "--api-key") {
            options.api_key = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--system-prompt") {
            options.system_prompt = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--append-system-prompt") {
            options.append_system_prompt.push_back(need_value(i, arg));
          } else if (arg == "--thinking") {
            options.thinking = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--print" || arg == "-p") {
            options.print = true;
            if (i + 1 < argc && argv[i + 1][0] != '-') options.prompts.push_back(argv[++i]);
          } else if (arg == "--continue" || arg == "-c") {
            options.continue_session = true;
          } else if (arg == "--session") {
            options.session_path = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--session-id") {
            options.session_id = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--session-dir") {
            options.session_dir = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--fork") {
            options.fork_path = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--no-session") {
            options.no_session = true;
          } else if (arg == "--name" || arg == "-n") {
            options.session_name = need_value(i, arg);
          } else if (arg == "--tools" || arg == "-t") {
            for (const std::string& tool : split_list(need_value(i, arg))) options.tool_allow.push_back(tool);
          } else if (arg == "--exclude-tools" || arg == "-xt") {
            for (const std::string& tool : split_list(need_value(i, arg))) options.tool_exclude.push_back(tool);
          } else if (arg == "--no-tools" || arg == "-nt") {
            options.no_tools = true;
          } else if (arg == "--no-builtin-tools" || arg == "-nbt") {
            options.no_builtin_tools = true;
          } else if (arg == "--max-turns") {
            options.max_turns = std::atoi(need_value(i, arg).c_str());
          } else if (arg == "--compact-threshold") {
            options.compact_threshold = std::atoll(need_value(i, arg).c_str());
          } else if (arg == "--tui") {
            options.tui = true;
          } else if (arg == "--verbose") {
            options.verbose = true;
          } else if (!arg.empty() && arg[0] == '-' && arg != "-") {
            std::cerr << "error: unknown option " << arg << "\n";
            std::exit(2);
          } else {
            options.prompts.push_back(arg);
          }
        }
        return options;
      }
      
      int run_cli(const CliOptions& options) {
        if (options.help) {
          print_help();
          return 0;
        }
        if (options.version) {
          std::cout << version_string() << "\n";
          return 0;
        }
        if (options.list_models) {
          std::cout << "Providers (use --provider with --model):\n";
          for (const std::string& provider : known_providers()) {
            const ModelSpec spec = resolve_model_spec(provider, "", "");
            std::cout << "  " << provider << " (default: " << spec.model << ")\n";
          }
          return 0;
        }
      
        const std::string cwd = current_working_directory();
      
        std::vector<ToolDefinition> tools;
        if (!options.no_tools && !options.no_builtin_tools) {
          for (ToolDefinition& tool : builtin_tools()) {
            if (tool_allowed(tool.name, options)) tools.push_back(std::move(tool));
          }
        }
      
        ModelSpec spec = resolve_model_spec(options.provider, options.model, options.api_key);
        spec.thinking = options.thinking;
        spec.max_tokens = 8192;
      
        const std::string directory = Session::directory_for_cwd(cwd, options.session_dir);
      
        std::unique_ptr<Session> session;
        if (!options.no_session) {
          std::string error;
          if (!options.session_path.empty()) {
            session = Session::load(options.session_path, &error);
            if (!session) {
              std::cerr << "error: " << error << "\n";
              return 1;
            }
          } else if (options.continue_session) {
            const std::string latest = Session::find_latest(cwd, directory);
            if (!latest.empty()) session = Session::load(latest, &error);
            if (!session) session = Session::create(cwd, directory, options.session_name, &error);
          } else if (!options.fork_path.empty()) {
            auto source = Session::load(options.fork_path, &error);
            if (!source) {
              std::cerr << "error: " << error << "\n";
              return 1;
            }
            session = Session::create(cwd, directory, options.session_name, &error, "", source->path());
            if (session) {
              for (const Json& message : source->record().messages) session->append_message(message);
            }
          } else if (!options.session_id.empty()) {
            const std::string existing = Session::find_by_id(options.session_id, directory);
            if (!existing.empty()) {
              session = Session::load(existing, &error);
            } else {
              session = Session::create(cwd, directory, options.session_name, &error, options.session_id);
            }
          } else {
            session = Session::create(cwd, directory, options.session_name, &error);
          }
          if (!session) {
            std::cerr << "warning: session disabled: " << error << "\n";
          }
        }
      
        // Resume the model recorded in the session unless the user chose one.
        if (session && !options.model_set && !session->record().model.empty()) {
          const std::string model = session->record().model;
          const std::string provider =
              options.provider_set ? options.provider : session->record().provider;
          spec = resolve_model_spec(provider, model, options.api_key);
          spec.thinking = options.thinking;
        }
        if (session && (session->record().provider != spec.provider || session->record().model != spec.model)) {
          session->append_model_change(spec.provider, spec.model);
        }
      
        std::string system_prompt =
            build_system_prompt(tools, cwd, options.system_prompt, options.append_system_prompt);
      
        AgentOptions agent_options;
        agent_options.model = spec;
        agent_options.system_prompt = std::move(system_prompt);
        agent_options.tools = tools;
        agent_options.cwd = cwd;
        agent_options.max_turns = options.max_turns;
        agent_options.compact_threshold = options.compact_threshold;
      
        const bool interactive = !options.print && options.prompts.empty() && options.mode == "text";
      
      #ifdef KARTA_TUI
        // Bare `karta` opens the terminal UI; pipes and prompts keep the plain modes.
        if (interactive && (options.tui || stdout_is_tty())) {
          TuiAppOptions tui_options;
          tui_options.agent = std::move(agent_options);
          tui_options.session = session.get();
          return run_tui_app(std::move(tui_options));
        }
      #endif
      #ifndef KARTA_TUI
        if (options.tui) {
          std::cerr << "error: this build has no terminal UI; rebuild with -DKARTA_BUILD_TUI=ON\n";
          return 2;
        }
      #endif
      
        Printer printer(options, interactive);
        const std::string model_label = spec.model.rfind(spec.provider + "/", 0) == 0
                                            ? spec.model
                                            : spec.provider + "/" + spec.model;
        printer.banner(model_label, cwd);
        if (options.verbose) {
          std::cerr << "karta: model=" << spec.provider << "/" << spec.model
                    << " tools=" << tools.size() << " session="
                    << (session ? session->path() : std::string("(ephemeral)")) << "\n";
        }
        if (spec.api_key.empty() && spec.provider != "ollama" && spec.provider != "lmstudio") {
          std::cerr << "warning: no API key found for " << spec.provider
                    << "; set the provider's env var or pass --api-key\n";
        }
      
        Agent agent(
            agent_options, [&](const AgentEvent& event) {
              if (event.type == "message_end") {
                if (session) session->append_message(event.payload);
              } else if (event.type == "compaction") {
                if (session) {
                  session->append_compaction(event.payload["summary"].as_string(),
                                             event.payload["tokensBefore"].as_int(0));
                }
              }
              printer.event(event);
            });
      
        if (session) {
          agent.set_messages(session->record().messages);
        }
      
        g_interrupt_target = agent.interrupt_flag();
        std::signal(SIGINT, handle_sigint);
      
        int status = 0;
      
        if (options.mode == "rpc") {
          std::mutex mutex;
          std::condition_variable condition;
          std::deque<std::string> prompts;
          bool eof = false;
      
          std::thread reader([&]() {
            std::string line;
            while (std::getline(std::cin, line)) {
              const std::string trimmed = trim(line);
              if (trimmed.empty()) continue;
              std::string parse_error;
              const Json command = Json::parse(trimmed, &parse_error);
              if (!parse_error.empty()) continue;
              const std::string type = command["type"].as_string();
              const std::string text = command["text"].as_string();
              if (type == "prompt") {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
                prompts.push_back(text);
                condition.notify_one();
              } else if (type == "steer") {
                agent.queue_steering(text);
              } else if (type == "followup" || type == "follow_up") {
                agent.queue_followup(text);
              } else if (type == "abort" || type == "interrupt") {
                agent.interrupt();
              }
            }
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
            eof = true;
            condition.notify_one();
          });
      
          while (true) {
            std::string prompt;
            {
              std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
              condition.wait(lock, [&] { return !prompts.empty() || eof; });
              if (prompts.empty() && eof) break;
              prompt = prompts.front();
              prompts.pop_front();
            }
            agent.reset_interrupt();
            agent.prompt(prompt);
          }
          reader.join();
        } else if (!options.prompts.empty()) {
          for (const std::string& prompt : options.prompts) {
            agent.reset_interrupt();
            agent.prompt(prompt);
            if (options.mode == "text") std::cout << "\n";
          }
        } else {
          std::string line;
          while (true) {
            std::cout << "\n› " << std::flush;
            if (!std::getline(std::cin, line)) break;
            const std::string trimmed = trim(line);
            if (trimmed.empty()) continue;
            if (trimmed == "/exit" || trimmed == "/quit") break;
            if (trimmed == "/help") {
              std::cout << "/exit quit · /session show session file · /model show model\n";
              continue;
            }
            if (trimmed == "/session") {
              std::cout << (session ? session->path() : std::string("(ephemeral session)")) << "\n";
              continue;
            }
            if (trimmed == "/model") {
              std::cout << spec.provider << "/" << spec.model << "\n";
              continue;
            }
            agent.reset_interrupt();
            agent.prompt(trimmed);
            if (agent.interrupted()) std::cerr << "\n[interrupted]\n";
          }
        }
      
        g_interrupt_target = nullptr;
        return status;
      }
      
      }  // namespace karta
      
- path: karta/src/agent.cpp
  content: |
      #include "agent.h"
      
      #include <algorithm>
      #include <sstream>
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      std::string block_text(const Json& message) {
        std::string text;
        for (const Json& block : message["content"].items()) {
          if (block["type"].as_string() == "text") text += block["text"].as_string();
        }
        return text;
      }
      
      std::vector<Json> tool_calls_of(const Json& message) {
        std::vector<Json> calls;
        for (const Json& block : message["content"].items()) {
          if (block["type"].as_string() == "toolCall") calls.push_back(block);
        }
        return calls;
      }
      
      Json message_list(const std::vector<Json>& messages) {
        Json out = Json::array();
        for (const Json& message : messages) out.push_back(message);
        return out;
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      Agent::Agent(AgentOptions options, EventSink sink)
          : options_(std::move(options)), sink_(std::move(sink)) {}
      
      void Agent::emit(const std::string& type, Json payload) {
        if (!sink_) return;
        AgentEvent event;
        event.type = type;
        event.payload = std::move(payload);
        sink_(event);
      }
      
      std::string Agent::model_label() const {
        return options_.model.provider + "/" + options_.model.model;
      }
      
      void Agent::queue_steering(std::string text) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        steering_queue_.push_back(std::move(text));
      }
      
      void Agent::queue_followup(std::string text) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
        followup_queue_.push_back(std::move(text));
      }
      
      std::vector<Json> Agent::drain_steering() {
        std::vector<std::string> queued;
        {
          std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
          queued.swap(steering_queue_);
        }
        std::vector<Json> messages;
        for (std::string& text : queued) messages.push_back(make_user_message(text));
        return messages;
      }
      
      std::vector<Json> Agent::drain_followups() {
        std::vector<std::string> queued;
        {
          std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
          queued.swap(followup_queue_);
        }
        std::vector<Json> messages;
        for (std::string& text : queued) messages.push_back(make_user_message(text));
        return messages;
      }
      
      std::vector<Json> Agent::prompt(const std::string& text, bool emit_agent_start) {
        std::vector<Json> produced;
        Json message = make_user_message(text);
        messages_.push_back(message);
        produced.push_back(message);
      
        if (emit_agent_start) emit("agent_start", Json::object());
        emit("turn_start", Json::object());
        emit("message_start", message);
        emit("message_end", message);
        run_loop(produced);
        return produced;
      }
      
      void Agent::run_loop(std::vector<Json>& produced) {
        std::vector<Json> pending = drain_steering();
        bool explicit_continuation = false;
      
        while (true) {
          bool has_more_tool_calls = true;
      
          // Inner loop: process tool calls and steering messages.
          while (has_more_tool_calls || !pending.empty()) {
            if (options_.max_turns > 0 && turn_count_ >= options_.max_turns) {
              Json error = Json::object();
              error.set("message", "Maximum turn count reached (" + std::to_string(options_.max_turns) + ")");
              emit("error", error);
              emit("agent_end", Json::object());
              return;
            }
      
            for (const Json& message : pending) {
              messages_.push_back(message);
              produced.push_back(message);
              emit("message_start", message);
              emit("message_end", message);
            }
            pending.clear();
      
            emit("turn_start", Json::object());
      
            LlmRequest request;
            request.system = options_.system_prompt;
            request.messages = messages_;
            request.tools.clear();
            for (const ToolDefinition& tool : options_.tools) request.tools.push_back(tool_declaration(tool));
      
            Json skeleton = Json::object();
            skeleton.set("role", "assistant");
            skeleton.set("content", Json::array());
            emit("message_start", skeleton);
      
            const AssistantTurn turn = stream_completion(
                options_.model, request,
                [&](const std::string& delta) {
                  Json update = Json::object();
                  update.set("delta", delta);
                  emit("message_update", update);
                },
                &interrupted_);
      
            const Json assistant = make_assistant_message(turn);
            messages_.push_back(assistant);
            produced.push_back(assistant);
            emit("message_end", assistant);
            turn_count_++;
      
            if (turn.stop_reason == "error" || turn.stop_reason == "aborted") {
              Json payload = Json::object();
              payload.set("message", assistant);
              payload.set("toolResults", Json::array());
              emit("turn_end", payload);
              if (!turn.error.empty() && turn.stop_reason == "error") {
                Json error = Json::object();
                error.set("message", turn.error);
                emit("error", error);
              }
              emit("agent_end", Json::object());
              return;
            }
      
            std::vector<Json> tool_results;
            const std::vector<Json> calls = tool_calls_of(assistant);
            has_more_tool_calls = false;
            bool terminate = false;
            if (!calls.empty()) {
              ToolBatch batch = turn.stop_reason == "length" ? fail_truncated_tool_calls(assistant)
                                                             : execute_tool_calls(assistant);
              tool_results = batch.messages;
              terminate = batch.terminate;
              has_more_tool_calls = !terminate;
              for (const Json& result : tool_results) {
                messages_.push_back(result);
                produced.push_back(result);
                emit("message_start", result);
                emit("message_end", result);
              }
            }
      
            Json turn_end = Json::object();
            turn_end.set("message", assistant);
            turn_end.set("toolResults", message_list(tool_results));
            emit("turn_end", turn_end);
      
            if (terminate) {
              emit("agent_end", Json::object());
              return;
            }
      
            maybe_compact();
      
            pending = drain_steering();
            if (has_more_tool_calls || !pending.empty()) explicit_continuation = false;
          }
      
          // Agent would stop here. Check for follow-up messages.
          std::vector<Json> followups = drain_followups();
          if (!followups.empty()) {
            explicit_continuation = false;
            pending = std::move(followups);
            continue;
          }
      
          // Fulfil an explicit continue decision with one context-only turn.
          if (explicit_continuation) {
            explicit_continuation = false;
            continue;
          }
          break;
        }
      
        emit("agent_end", Json::object());
      }
      
      Agent::ToolBatch Agent::execute_tool_calls(const Json& assistant_message) {
        ToolBatch batch;
        ToolContext context{options_.cwd};
      
        for (const Json& call : tool_calls_of(assistant_message)) {
          const std::string id = call["id"].as_string();
          const std::string name = call["name"].as_string();
      
          Json arguments = Json::object();
          std::string parse_error;
          if (call["arguments"].is_string()) {
            const std::string raw = call["arguments"].as_string();
            if (!raw.empty()) arguments = Json::parse(raw, &parse_error);
          } else if (call["arguments"].is_object()) {
            arguments = call["arguments"];
          }
      
          Json start = Json::object();
          start.set("toolCallId", id);
          start.set("toolName", name);
          start.set("arguments", arguments);
          emit("tool_execution_start", start);
      
          ToolOutcome outcome;
          if (!parse_error.empty()) {
            outcome.text = "Invalid tool arguments: " + parse_error;
            outcome.is_error = true;
          } else {
            const ToolDefinition* tool = find_tool(options_.tools, name);
            if (!tool) {
              outcome.text = "Unknown tool: " + name;
              outcome.is_error = true;
            } else {
              try {
                outcome = tool->execute(arguments, context);
              } catch (const std::exception& error) {
                outcome.text = std::string("Tool failed: ") + error.what();
                outcome.is_error = true;
              }
            }
          }
      
          const Json message = make_tool_result_message(id, name, outcome.text, outcome.is_error);
          batch.messages.push_back(message);
      
          Json end = Json::object();
          end.set("toolCallId", id);
          end.set("toolName", name);
          end.set("isError", outcome.is_error);
          end.set("result", outcome.text);
          emit("tool_execution_end", end);
      
          if (outcome.terminate) {
            batch.terminate = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        return batch;
      }
      
      Agent::ToolBatch Agent::fail_truncated_tool_calls(const Json& assistant_message) {
        ToolBatch batch;
        for (const Json& call : tool_calls_of(assistant_message)) {
          const std::string id = call["id"].as_string();
          const std::string name = call["name"].as_string();
          const std::string text =
              "Tool call not executed: the response hit the model's output limit, so its arguments may "
              "be truncated. Re-issue the call with a smaller response.";
          batch.messages.push_back(make_tool_result_message(id, name, text, true));
      
          Json end = Json::object();
          end.set("toolCallId", id);
          end.set("toolName", name);
          end.set("isError", true);
          end.set("result", text);
          emit("tool_execution_end", end);
        }
        return batch;
      }
      
      long long Agent::estimate_tokens() const {
        std::size_t characters = options_.system_prompt.size();
        for (const Json& message : messages_) {
          characters += block_text(message).size();
          for (const Json& block : message["content"].items()) {
            if (block["type"].as_string() == "toolCall") characters += block["arguments"].as_string().size();
          }
        }
        return static_cast<long long>(characters / 4);
      }
      
      void Agent::maybe_compact() {
        if (options_.compact_threshold <= 0 || compaction_failed_) return;
        const long long tokens_before = estimate_tokens();
        if (tokens_before < options_.compact_threshold) return;
      
        std::string transcript;
        for (const Json& message : messages_) {
          std::string role = message["role"].as_string();
          if (role == "toolResult") role = "tool";
          transcript += role + ": " + block_text(message);
          for (const Json& block : message["content"].items()) {
            if (block["type"].as_string() == "toolCall") {
              transcript += "\n[tool call] " + block["name"].as_string() + " " +
                            block["arguments"].as_string().substr(0, 400);
            }
          }
          transcript += "\n\n";
        }
      
        LlmRequest request;
        request.system =
            "You compact conversation history for a coding agent. Preserve the user's goal, decisions, "
            "files and symbols touched, commands run, errors and their fixes, and anything still "
            "unfinished. Be specific and terse. Reply with the summary only.";
        request.messages.push_back(
            make_user_message("Summarize this transcript so work can continue without it:\n\n" + transcript));
      
        const AssistantTurn turn =
            stream_completion(options_.model, request, nullptr, &interrupted_);
        if (turn.stop_reason == "error" || turn.stop_reason == "aborted" || turn.text.empty()) {
          compaction_failed_ = true;
          Json failure = Json::object();
          failure.set("message", turn.error.empty() ? "compaction produced no summary" : turn.error);
          emit("compaction_failed", failure);
          return;
        }
      
        compaction_failed_ = false;
        Json event = Json::object();
        event.set("summary", turn.text);
        event.set("tokensBefore", tokens_before);
        emit("compaction", event);
      
        messages_.clear();
        messages_.push_back(make_user_message(
            "[Context summary: the earlier conversation was compacted]\n\n" + turn.text));
      }
      
      }  // namespace karta

      #include "tui/app.h"
      
      #include <algorithm>
      #include <atomic>
      #include <cctype>
      #include <chrono>
      #include <cstdlib>
      #include <deque>
      #include <iostream>
      #include <memory>
      #include <mutex>
      #include <string>
      #include <thread>
      #include <utility>
      #include <vector>
      
      #include "auth.h"
      #include "json.h"
      #include "provider.h"
      #include "session.h"
      #include "tui/model-picker.h"
      #include "tui/screen.h"
      
      namespace karta {
      namespace {
      
      constexpr const char* kVersion = "0.1.0";
      constexpr const char* kReadyStatus = "ready · /login · /model";
      
      std::string trim(const std::string& value) {
        const std::size_t begin = value.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
        if (begin == std::string::npos) return "";
        const std::size_t end = value.find_last_not_of(" \t\r\n");
        return value.substr(begin, end - begin + 1);
      }
      
      std::string lower(std::string value) {
        for (char& c : value) c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
        return value;
      }
      
      bool all_digits(const std::string& value) {
        if (value.empty()) return false;
        for (char c : value) {
          if (c < '0' || c > '9') return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
      
      std::string first_line(const std::string& text, std::size_t limit) {
        const std::size_t newline = text.find('\n');
        std::string line = newline == std::string::npos ? text : text.substr(0, newline);
        if (line.size() > limit) line = line.substr(0, limit) + "…";
        return line;
      }
      
      std::string join(const std::vector<std::string>& items, const std::string& separator) {
        std::string out;
        for (const std::string& item : items) {
          if (!out.empty()) out += separator;
          out += item;
        }
        return out;
      }
      
      }  // namespace
      
      class TuiApp {
       public:
        explicit TuiApp(TuiAppOptions options) : options_(std::move(options)), model_picker_(nullptr, nullptr) {}
      
        ~TuiApp() {
          if (agent_) agent_->interrupt();
          job_cancel_ = true;
          if (worker_.joinable()) worker_.join();
          if (job_thread_.joinable()) job_thread_.join();
          model_picker_.cancel_fetch();
          ui_.stop();
        }
      
        int run();
      
       private:
        enum class Phase { Chat, LoginProvider, LoginKey, ModelPick };
        enum class JobKind { None, FetchModels };
      
        void sync_header();
        void reset_prompt();
        void say(const std::string& text, tui::Style style);
      
        void handle_key(tui::Key key, int ch);
        void handle_submit(const std::string& line);
        void handle_command(const std::string& line);
      
        void start_prompt(const std::string& text);
        bool consume_events();
        void stop_worker();
      
        void start_job(JobKind kind, const ModelSpec& spec);
        bool consume_job();
      
        void begin_login(const std::string& provider);
        void finish_login_provider(const std::string& name);
        void finish_login_key(const std::string& key);
      
        void begin_model_pick();
        void show_models();
        void finish_model_pick(const std::string& selection);
      
        void apply_spec(const ModelSpec& spec);
        void apply_model(const std::string& model);
        ModelSpec spec_for(const std::string& provider, const std::string& model) const;
      
        TuiAppOptions options_;
        tui::Tui ui_;
        std::unique_ptr<Agent> agent_;
        tui::ModelPicker model_picker_;
      
        bool running_ = true;
        bool busy_ = false;
        Phase phase_ = Phase::Chat;
      
        std::thread worker_;
        std::atomic<bool> worker_done_{false};
        std::mutex queue_mutex_;
        std::deque<AgentEvent> queue_;
      
        JobKind job_kind_ = JobKind::None;
        std::thread job_thread_;
        std::atomic<bool> job_cancel_{false};
        std::atomic<bool> job_done_{false};
        std::atomic<bool> job_running_{false};
        std::mutex job_mutex_;
        std::string job_error_;
        std::vector<std::string> job_models_;
      
        std::string pending_provider_;
        std::vector<std::string> models_;
      };
      
      int TuiApp::run() {
        if (!ui_.init()) {
          std::cerr << "error: failed to initialize the terminal UI "
                       "(run outside a pipe, or use --mode text -p)\n";
          return 1;
        }
      
        agent_ = std::make_unique<Agent>(options_.agent, [this](const AgentEvent& event) {
          std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
          queue_.push_back(event);
        });
        if (options_.session) agent_->set_messages(options_.session->record().messages);
      
        sync_header();
        say("karta " + std::string(kVersion) + " — terminal UI", tui::Style::Accent);
        say("/login connect a provider · /model choose a model · /help commands · /exit quit",
            tui::Style::Dim);
      
        const ModelSpec& current = options_.agent.model;
        if (!provider_is_local(current.provider) && current.api_key.empty()) {
          say("Not connected. Type /login to add an API key for a provider.", tui::Style::Dim);
        } else {
          say("Connected to " + current.provider + " · model " + current.model, tui::Style::Dim);
        }
        ui_.set_status(kReadyStatus, false);
      
        while (running_) {
          bool changed = consume_events();
          changed = consume_job() || changed;
      
          int ch = 0;
          const tui::Key key = ui_.poll(&ch);
          if (key != tui::Key::None) {
            handle_key(key, ch);
            changed = true;
          }
      
          if (changed) {
            ui_.draw();
          } else {
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(20));
          }
        }
      
        stop_worker();
        job_cancel_ = true;
        if (job_thread_.joinable()) job_thread_.join();
        ui_.stop();
        return 0;
      }
      
      void TuiApp::say(const std::string& text, tui::Style style) {
        ui_.append_text(text, style);
      }
      
      void TuiApp::sync_header() {
        tui::SessionInfo info;
        info.title = "karta";
        info.version = kVersion;
        info.cwd = options_.agent.cwd;
        info.provider = options_.agent.model.provider;
        info.model = options_.agent.model.model;
        if (options_.session) info.id = options_.session->id();
        ui_.set_header(info);
      }
      
      void TuiApp::reset_prompt() {
        phase_ = Phase::Chat;
        ui_.set_prompt("> ");
        ui_.set_input("");
        ui_.set_input_hidden(false);
        ui_.set_status(kReadyStatus, false);
      }
      
      void TuiApp::handle_key(tui::Key key, int /*ch*/) {
        switch (key) {
          case tui::Key::Enter: {
            const std::string line = ui_.input();
            ui_.set_input("");
            handle_submit(line);
            return;
          }
          case tui::Key::CtrlC:
          case tui::Key::Escape:
            if (busy_ && agent_) {
              agent_->interrupt();
              ui_.set_status("cancelling…", true);
            } else if (job_running_) {
              // A model fetch in flight: abort the HTTP transfer.
              job_cancel_ = true;
              ui_.set_status("cancelling…", true);
            } else if (phase_ != Phase::Chat) {
              reset_prompt();
              say("cancelled", tui::Style::Dim);
            } else if (key == tui::Key::CtrlC) {
              running_ = false;
            }
            return;
          default:
            return;
        }
      }
      
      void TuiApp::handle_submit(const std::string& line) {
        if (line.empty()) return;
      
        // Commands are recognized in every phase, so /exit always quits and a
        // mistyped slash can never be stored as a provider, key, or model id.
        if (line[0] == '/') {
          handle_command(line);
          return;
        }
      
        if (phase_ == Phase::LoginProvider) {
          finish_login_provider(line);
          return;
        }
        if (phase_ == Phase::LoginKey) {
          finish_login_key(line);
          return;
        }
        if (phase_ == Phase::ModelPick) {
          finish_model_pick(line);
          return;
        }
        if (job_running_) {
          say("still fetching models — Esc cancels", tui::Style::Dim);
          return;
        }
        start_prompt(line);
      }
      
      void TuiApp::handle_command(const std::string& line) {
        std::string body = line.substr(1);
        std::string name = body;
        std::string argument;
        const std::size_t space = body.find_first_of(" \t");
        if (space != std::string::npos) {
          name = body.substr(0, space);
          argument = trim(body.substr(space + 1));
        }
        name = lower(name);
      
        if (name == "exit" || name == "quit") {
          running_ = false;
          return;
        }
        if (name == "help") {
          say("Commands", tui::Style::Bold);
          say("  /login [provider]   connect a provider with an API key", tui::Style::Normal);
          say("  /logout [provider]  forget the saved key for a provider", tui::Style::Normal);
          say("  /model [id]         list and pick a model (or set one directly)", tui::Style::Normal);
          say("  /provider [name]    show or switch the provider", tui::Style::Normal);
          say("  /session            show the session file", tui::Style::Normal);
          say("  /clear              clear the transcript", tui::Style::Normal);
          say("  /exit               quit (Esc cancels a running turn)", tui::Style::Normal);
          return;
        }
        if (name == "login") {
          begin_login(argument);
          return;
        }
        if (name == "model" || name == "models") {
          if (!argument.empty()) {
            const ModelSpec& current = options_.agent.model;
            if (!provider_is_local(current.provider) && current.api_key.empty()) {
              say("not logged in to " + current.provider + " — use /login first", tui::Style::Error);
              return;
            }
            if (all_digits(argument)) {
              if (models_.empty()) {
                say("run /model first to load the model list", tui::Style::Dim);
                return;
              }
              finish_model_pick(argument);
              return;
            }
            apply_model(argument);
            return;
          }
          begin_model_pick();
          return;
        }
        if (name == "provider") {
          if (argument.empty()) {
            say("provider: " + options_.agent.model.provider, tui::Style::Normal);
          } else {
            begin_login(argument);
          }
          return;
        }
        if (name == "logout") {
          const std::string provider =
              argument.empty() ? options_.agent.model.provider : lower(trim(argument));
          if (provider_is_local(provider)) {
            say(provider + " does not need credentials", tui::Style::Dim);
            return;
          }
          const bool removed = remove_auth_key(provider);
          if (provider == options_.agent.model.provider) {
            ModelSpec spec = resolve_model_spec(provider, options_.agent.model.model, "");
            apply_spec(spec);
            if (!spec.api_key.empty()) {
              say("an environment variable still provides a key for " + provider, tui::Style::Dim);
            }
          }
          say(removed ? "logged out of " + provider + " — saved key removed"
                      : "no saved key for " + provider,
              removed ? tui::Style::Success : tui::Style::Dim);
          return;
        }
        if (name == "session") {
          say("session: " + (options_.session ? options_.session->path() : std::string("(ephemeral)")),
              tui::Style::Normal);
          return;
        }
        if (name == "clear") {
          ui_.clear_transcript();
          return;
        }
```

```

- path: karta/CMakeLists.txt
  content: |
      cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.16)
      project(karta LANGUAGES CXX)
      
      set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17)
      set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
      set(CMAKE_CXX_EXTENSIONS OFF)
      
      if(NOT CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE)
        set(CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE Release)
      endif()
      
      # Optional terminal UI (ncurses). When enabled, launching `karta` with no
      # prompt opens the TUI; /login and /model live there.
      option(KARTA_BUILD_TUI "Build the terminal UI (ncurses)" OFF)
      
      add_executable(karta
        src/main.cpp
        src/json.cpp
        src/platform.cpp
        src/http.cpp
        src/provider.cpp
        src/auth.cpp
        src/tools.cpp
        src/agent.cpp
        src/session.cpp
        src/prompt.cpp
        src/cli.cpp
      )
      
      target_compile_options(karta PRIVATE -Wall -Wextra -Wno-unused-parameter)
      target_include_directories(karta PRIVATE src)
      
      find_package(CURL REQUIRED)
      find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
      target_link_libraries(karta PRIVATE CURL::libcurl Threads::Threads)
      
      if(KARTA_BUILD_TUI)
        find_package(PkgConfig REQUIRED)
        pkg_check_modules(NCURSES ncurses REQUIRED)
      
        target_compile_definitions(karta PRIVATE KARTA_TUI)
        target_include_directories(karta PRIVATE ${NCURSES_INCLUDE_DIRS})
        target_link_libraries(karta PRIVATE ${NCURSES_LIBRARIES})
        target_sources(karta PRIVATE    src/tui/screen.cpp
        src/tui/screen.h
        src/tui/app.cpp
        src/tui/app.h
        src/tui/model-picker.cpp
        src/tui/model-picker.h
        src/tui/select-list.cpp
        src/tui/select-list.h
        )
        message(STATUS "TUI enabled - `karta` opens the terminal UI when attached to a TTY")
      else()
        message(STATUS "TUI disabled - pass -DKARTA_BUILD_TUI=ON to build the terminal UI")
      endif()
      
      install(TARGETS karta RUNTIME DESTINATION bin)
      
```

```
# Set environment variables for the build.

# The shell in which to execute make rules.

# The CMake executable.

# The command to remove a file.

# Escaping for special characters.
```

```
std::string random_id() {
  static std::mt19937_64 generator(std::random_device{}());
  static const char alphabet[] = "0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
  std::string out;
  for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) out.push_back(alphabet[generator() % 36]);
  return out;
}
```

```
    const bool changed = selected_index_ != clicked_index;
    selected_index_ = clicked_index;
    if (changed) notify_selection_change();
    const SelectItem* selected_item = filtered_items_.empty() ? nullptr
                                                              : &filtered_items_[selected_index_];
    if (selected_item) on_select_.dispatch(*selected_item);
  }
}

void SelectList::handle_input(const std::string& data) {
  const KeybindingsManager& kb = get_keybindings();
  const int page_index = get_select_page_index(kb, data, selected_index_, filtered_items_.size(),
                                               max_visible_);

  if (kb.matches(data, "tui.select.up")) {
    selected_index_ = selected_index_ <= 0 ? static_cast<int>(filtered_items_.size()) - 1
                                             : selected_index_ - 1;
    notify_selection_change();
  } else if (kb.matches(data, "tui.select.down")) {
    selected_index_ = selected_index_ >= static_cast<int>(filtered_items_.size()) - 1 ? 0
                                                                                      : selected_index_ +
                                                                                          1;
    notify_selection_change();
  } else if (page_index != std::nullopt) {
    if (filtered_items_.empty() || page_index == selected_index_) return;
    selected_index_ = page_index;
    notify_selection_change();
  } else if (kb.matches(data, "tui.select.confirm")) {
    if (!filtered_items_.empty() && on_select_) {
      if (on_select_) on_select_(filtered_items_[selected_index_]);
    }
  } else if (kb.matches(data, "tui.select.cancel")) {
    if (on_cancel_) on_cancel_();
  }
}

std::pair<int, int> SelectList::get_visible_range() const {
  if (filtered_items_.empty()) return {0, 0};
  const int center = selected_index_;
  const int half = max_visible_ / 2;
  int start_index = center - half;
  if (start_index < 0) start_index = 0;
  const int end_index = start_index + max_visible_;
  if (end_index > static_cast<int>(filtered_items_.size())) {
    start_index = filtered_items_.size() - max_visible_;
    if (start_index < 0) start_index = 0;
  }
  return {start_index, std::min(end_index, static_cast<int>(filtered_items_.size()))};
}

int SelectList::get_primary_column_width(int width) const {
  const int requested_min = layout_.min_primary_column_width.value_or(kDefaultPrimaryWidth);
  const int requested_max = layout_.max_primary_column_width.value_or(kDefaultPrimaryWidth);
  const int min = std::max(1, std::min(requested_min, requested_max));
  const int max = std::max(1, std::max(requested_min, requested_max));

  int widest = 0;
  for (const auto& item : filtered_items_) {
    widest = std::max(widest, static_cast<int>(visible_width(get_display_value(item))));
  }
  widest += 2;  // primary column gap

  const int column_width = std::max(min, std::min(widest, max));
  return column_width;
}

std::string SelectList::get_display_value(const SelectItem& item) const {
  return item.label.empty() ? item.value : item.label;
}
```

```
  const int primary_column_width = get_primary_column_width(actual_width);

  const auto [start_index, end_index] = get_visible_range();
  for (int i = start_index; i < end_index; ++i) {
    const SelectItem& item = filtered_items_[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
    const bool is_selected = i == selected_index_;
    const std::string description =
        item.description ? normalize_to_single_line(item.description) : std::string();
    lines.push_back(render_item(item, is_selected, actual_width, description,
                                primary_column_width));
  }

  if (start_index > 0 || end_index < static_cast<int>(filtered_items_.size())) {
    std::string scroll_text =
        "  (" + std::to_string(selected_index_ + 1) + "/" +
        std::to_string(filtered_items_.size()) + ")";
    lines.push_back(theme_.scroll_info(truncate_to_width(scroll_text,
                                                       actual_width - 2, "")));
  }

  return lines;
}

void SelectList::handle_mouse(MouseEvent event) {
  if (filtered_items_.empty()) return;

  if (event.type == MouseEventType::Wheel && event.wheel_delta != 0) {
    const int delta = event.wheel_delta < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    const int previous_index = selected_index_;
    selected_index_ = std::max(0, std::min(
        static_cast<int>(filtered_items_.size()) - 1, selected_index_ + delta));
    if (selected_index_ != previous_index) notify_selection_change();
    return;
  }

  // Hover must not change selection: the visible range is centered on it.
  if (event.button != MouseButton::Left || (event.type != MouseEventType::Press &&
                                             event.type != MouseEventType::Click))
```

```
92: private:
---

class SelectList {
 public:
  using OnSelect = std::function<void(const SelectItem&)>;
  using OnCancel = std::function<void()>;

  SelectList(std::vector<SelectItem> items, int max_visible, const SelectListTheme& theme,
             const SelectListLayoutOptions& layout = SelectListLayoutOptions{},
             const OnSelect& on_select = OnSelect());

  void set_on_select(const OnSelect& on_select) { on_select_ = on_select; }

  void set_filter(const std::string& filter);
  void set_selected_index(int index);
  void invalidate();

  // Renders the visible window as text lines at `width` columns.
  std::vector<std::string> render(std::size_t width) const;

  // Mouse events (only left button press/click/wheel are handled).
  void handle_mouse(MouseEvent event);

  // Keyboard events forwarded from the keybindings manager.
  void handle_input(const std::string& data);

  void set_on_select(OnSelect on_select) { on_select_ = std::move(on_select); }
  void set_on_cancel(OnCancel on_cancel) { on_cancel_ = std::move(on_cancel); }

  const std::vector<SelectItem>& items() const { return items_; }
  const std::vector<SelectItem>& filtered_items() const { return filtered_items_; }
  int selected_index() const { return selected_index_; }

 private:
  std::vector<std::string> render_item(const SelectItem& item, bool is_selected,
                                       int width, const std::string& description,
                                       int primary_column_width) const;
  std::string get_display_value(const SelectItem& item) const;
  std::string truncate_primary(const SelectItem& item, bool is_selected, int max_width,
                               int column_width) const;
  std::pair<int, int> get_visible_range() const;
  int get_primary_column_width(int width) const;

  std::vector<SelectItem> items_;
  std::vector<SelectItem> filtered_items_;
  int selected_index_ = 0;
  int mouse_pressed_index_ = -1;
  int max_visible_ = 5;
  SelectListTheme theme_;
  SelectListLayoutOptions layout_;

  OnSelect on_select_;
  OnCancel on_cancel_;
};

}  // namespace karta::tui

```
```
// Karta — pi select-list component.
//
// Mirrors `reference/pi/packages/tui/src/components/select-list.ts`:
//  numbered primary column + optional description, bounded visible window
//  centered on the selection, filtering, mouse and keyboard navigation.
#pragma once

#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <functional>

#include "tui/screen.h"

namespace karta::tui {

struct SelectItem {
  std::string value;       // stored value / id
  std::string label;       // displayed primary text
  std::string description; // optional secondary line
};

struct SelectListTruncatePrimaryContext {
  std::string text;
  int max_width;
  int column_width;
  SelectItem item;
  bool is_selected;
};

struct SelectListLayoutOptions {
  std::optional<int> min_primary_column_width = std::nullopt;
  std::optional<int> max_primary_column_width = std::nullopt;
  std::optional<std::function<std::string(SelectListTruncatePrimaryContext)>>
      truncate_primary = std::nullopt;
};

struct SelectListTheme {
  std::function<std::string(const std::string&)> no_match = [](auto text) { return text; };
  std::function<std::string(const std::string&)> selected_text =
      [](auto text) { return text; };
  std::function<std::string(const std::string&)> description =
      [](auto text) { return text; };
  std::function<std::string(const std::string&)> scroll_info =
      [](auto text) { return text; };
};

// Event over mouse events to keep the component portable outside ncurses.
struct MouseEvent {
  enum class MouseEventType { Press, Release, Move, Drag, Click, Wheel };
  enum class MouseButton { Left, Middle, Right, None };
  MouseEventType type;
  MouseButton button = MouseButton::None;
  int x = 0;    // local to the component
  int y = 0;    // local to the component
  int width = 0;
  int height = 0;
  int wheel_delta = 0;
};
```
